# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-19)

## Corpus Check
- 3986 files · ~10,029,399 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 92 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 30, .css 16, .entitlements 11)

## Summary
- 58017 nodes · 163381 edges · 1249 communities (1158 shown, 91 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 5754 edges (avg confidence: 0.88)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `116f95ee`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
